- **~~VOX-24 · Voxel Isle: the Assessor's Policy~~** ✅ — **Round Twenty-Two's flagship lap is complete: six insurance pieces, one per game, every premium priced against a disaster the game's own infrastructure makes rare but can't zero.** The isle's leg insures the fields against the crows: a new 🛟 chip above the Cloud Wager's takes **25 🪙 to cover the crops till dawn — a crow peck that actually LANDS pays 50 in damages on the spot; a quiet day lapses the premium at the real day rollover**. The pricing logic closes the round's thesis: the scarecrow prevents raids in its radius and the Corn Crib blunts every bite to 0.15× — prevention the isle already sells — while the policy is for the peck that gets through anyway, settling inside `updateCrow`'s real peck site via a small `settleCropPolicy` seam. One cover at a time; no bet on a bird already sitting isn't needed — the claim requires a LANDED peck by construction; `cropPolicy`/`policyClaims`/`policyLapsed` ride the whole-state save for free; 🏆 **Acts of Crow** crowns four claims; the how-to gains its bullet. Verified one-shot with no hook needed (13 assertions: the 25/50 constants + registration + bullet + chip quote, a purchase at exactly −25 with the chip reading covered, the double-buy and short-coin refusals, **a REAL peck driving the claim** — `addPlant` → `spawnCrow` → sit out the 18s clock → exactly +50 with the tally ticking and the cover cleared, an uninsured peck paying nothing, **the REAL dawn rollover lapsing a quiet policy**, the four-claim crown, cover + ledger riding the save, legacy migration), audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~HVALE-26 · Hearthvale: the Guild Surety~~** ✅ — the insurance round takes the high road, and the teamster's departure event gains its other side. The Teamster's Odds bet ON a clean run; now **a guild clerk stands beside the box seat selling the opposite position: 10 gold of surety that pays exactly 45 on a waylaying — the precise gap between a robbed purse (25) and a clean one (70), so an insured caravan comes home whole either way** (`SURETY_PAY = CARAVAN_PAY − CARAVAN_ROBBED`, priced by definition, not by tuning). The departure modal now offers three positions on one roll of the road — stake it, insure it, or ride bare — and both instruments resolve on the same waylay roll in `caravanAtDawn`: a robbery pays the claim beside the dead stake, a clean road pays the stake beside the unclaimed premium, and the Chronicle keeps all four endings. Same round logic as everywhere: the watch and the beacon make waylayings RARE (a 96% clean read fully built), so the surety is priced for the 4% the infrastructure can't reach. `caravanSurety`/`suretyClaims` join the explicit save lists with migration ("older loads rode bare"); 🏆 **Made Whole** crowns three claims. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (10 assertions: the 10/45 constants with the gap identity checked (`pay === cpay − robbed`) + registration, the fresh-valley empty book, the clerk's offer at departure, the purchase at exactly −10 arming the surety, a pinned waylaying paying exactly 25+45 with the tally ticking and the Chronicle receipt, a pinned clean return paying only the 70 with the premium expiring unclaimed, the three-claim crown, a surety in flight + tally riding the save, legacy migration), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~HV-29 · Homeless Village: Marisol's Garage~~** ✅ — the insurance round comes to the camp, and it insures the one loss the camp's whole toolkit only ever SOFTENS: the sweep's confiscation. Packing up keeps 75%, the buried stash halves the take — but nothing has ever zeroed it, until now: **a new 🚙 Garage Favor on the action bar spends 2 goodwill to stow the camp's loose goods in Marisol's garage across the street, and the next sweep's confiscation simply finds nothing** — `keep` drops to 0 inside the sweep's own effect, the cover is spent the night it matters, and the log says where everything was. The honest limits are the design: **tents still fall, morale still takes the hit — the garage holds goods, not shelter** — and the favor rides the friendship loop like every Marisol interaction (`bumpRegular('marisol')` on the ask). One cover at a time; `garageCover`/`garageSaves` ride the whole-object save with migrations ("saves from before HV-29"); the goal ladder gains **garage2** (ride out two covered sweeps). Verified one-shot with no hook needed (11 assertions: the action + goal + cost-2 slate, a placement at exactly −2 goodwill arming the cover — survive-goal payouts primed first so deltas stay exact — the double-ask and short-goodwill refusals, a pinned covered sweep confiscating exactly nothing with the tally ticking and the tent still falling, a pinned uncovered sweep taking its usual cut, the goal value at two, the ledger riding the save, legacy migration), audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~AOW-33 · Age of War: the Rebuilder's Bond~~** ✅ — the insurance round reaches the wall, and it is the Ironside Wager's exact mirror: where the wager pays when the walls END WELL, the bond pays when they end badly. **From Age II, 250 gold (I, or the new 🛟 Bond button beside Wager) insures the base for the CURRENT wave at the hp it stands when signed — end the wave below that mark and the underwriter's masons rebuild half the loss on the spot; end at-or-above and the premium expires worthless, the outcome you were paying for.** Resolution rides the same wave's-turn seam as the wager — `resolveBond()` fires right after `resolveIronWager()` at the rollover, and the ordering matters: the wager settles on the wave's true final hp before the bond's masons touch anything, so the two instruments can be held together without gaming each other. The rebuild clamps at `playerBaseMax` (Masons-thickened walls included); one bond a wave; `runStats.bondsPaid/bondsExpired/bondHealed` book the run's ledger and reset clears it; 🏆 **Underwritten** crowns three claims in one run. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (11 assertions: the button beside Wager, the Age-I refusal + 250/half constants, the short-gold refusal, a funded bond at exactly −250 marking the signing hp, the one-a-wave gate, a 400-below finish rebuilding exactly 200 with the claim counted, an above-the-mark finish expiring with no heal, the max-clamp rebuild landing at exactly 20+485 under a 1000 cap, the three-claim crown, a fresh run clearing the ledger), `node --check` clean before and after the hook strip, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~LAB-31 · Grow Op: the Lawyer~~** ✅ — the insurance round reaches the corner, and it covers the one disaster the empire's whole security stack only makes RARER, never impossible: the sting. **A \$1,500 retainer (⚖️ button on the fixed row, Act II) buys exactly one case — the next badge still takes the bags, but the lawyer takes the department: their full list value comes back as damages and the 15-point heat spike is argued down to 8.** Then the retainer's spent, one case at a time. The design turn mirrors the whole round: the Lookout reads the wire, the burner halves the odds, the Informant sells certainty — all prevention — while the Lawyer is the first piece priced for the day prevention misses anyway. The claim resolves inside `sellToDealer`'s real sting branch (no side machinery), pays through `addCash` at `salePrice() × seized`, and books `state.casesWon`; `lawyer`/`casesWon` join the explicit save lists with legacy migration ("older corners had no counsel"); 🏆 **Habeas Corpus** crowns three cases. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (12 assertions: the 1500/8 constants + registration, the garage-hidden button, the Act II reveal + short-cash refusal, a funded retainer at exactly −\$1,500 reading RETAINED, the one-case-at-a-time refusal, an insured sting returning exactly list × seized with the spike at 8 and the retainer spent, an uninsured sting at the full 15 with nothing back, the three-case crown, a live retainer + count riding the save, legacy migration), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~TYC-33 · Startup Tycoon: the Hedge~~** ✅ — **Round Twenty-Two opens: the insurance round**, the wager round's mirror. Every leg this round sells an opt-in premium against the game's own signature disaster, resolved by the same live state the wagers read — and where the wager's edge was infrastructure that helps you WIN, the premium prices infrastructure that keeps you from LOSING. The Tycoon leg insures the race itself: post-IPO, while the race is live, a 🛟 chip beside the moonshot's writes **a hedge for 15 seconds of revenue (never under \$400) — if the rival rings the bell first, the underwriter pays 3×; ring it yourself and the premium expires worthless, which is the outcome you were paying for**. The claim pays inside the rival's own bell-ringing branch in `updateRival`; the expiry books inside `seasonGoalHit`'s flip in `updateGoalHud` — both resolution sites are the race's real endings, no side timers. One policy a race; the chip re-quotes the drifting premium every two seconds; `hedge` rides its own SAVE_SCHEMA entry (an active policy survives reload mid-race) with `hedgesPaid`/`hedgesBurned` lifetime books; 🏆 **The Safety Net** crowns three claims. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (12 assertions: the 3×/\$400 constants + registration, the garage-hidden chip, the decided-race and short-cash refusals, a funded policy at exactly the quoted premium with the chip reading covered, the one-at-a-time gate, a rival bell paying exactly 3× with the book at 1 claimed, an own bell burning the premium with no payout, the three-claim crown, an active policy + book riding the save, legacy migration), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~SITE · Round Twenty-One wrap: the wager round ships + eureka-v29 + QA-10~~** ✅ — the studio closes its twenty-first flagship round, **the wager round**: six opt-in stakes, one per game, every one resolved by live game state (the rival race's finish, the clean-sale streak, the wall's hp mark at the horn, the promoted forecast, the caravan's own waylay roll, the rain clock) and every edge a piece of infrastructure the player already built (the rubber-banded race read, the burner and the regulars, the healing kit and the Sappers, the crafted radio, the watch and the beacon, the weather vane). Patch notes lead with **Round Twenty-One** — 🚀 Moonshot, 🎲 Hot Hand, 🛡️ Ironside Wager, 🌧️ Rain Bet, 🐴 Teamster's Odds, ☁️ Cloud Wager — `SW_VERSION` rolls to **eureka-v29** so installed PWAs pick the whole round up, the `patchnotes` suite tracks the new lead, and all six README flagship blurbs weave in their wager clause. The battery grows **65 → 67**: `hvrainbet` and `voxcloud` (both hook-free) promoted from one-shots to standing suites, with coverage rows in tests/README.md. **QA-10**: the full 67-suite battery run end-to-end at the wrap, recorded in tests/README.md alongside the audit's 12/32 baseline.
- **~~VOX-23 · Voxel Isle: the Cloud Wager~~** ✅ — **Round Twenty-One's flagship lap is complete: six wagers, one per game, every one settled by live game state and priced by infrastructure the player already built.** The isle's leg bets on its own sky: a new 🎲 chip above the hotbar takes **30 🪙 that rain falls within 90 seconds, paying 60** — rain already falling takes no bet, one wager rides at a time, and the countdown lives on the chip. The edge is the 🌦️ Weather Vane's second payoff: the chip doesn't even appear until the vane is up (its early call — showers announced half a minute out — turns a blind gamble on the season into a read), so the wager is literally gated on the infrastructure that beats it. A win pays through `grantCoins` with the toast counting the streak; a dry window blows the stake away. `cloudWins`/`cloudLosses` ride the whole-state save for free; 🏆 **Cloud Reader** crowns six wins. Verified one-shot with no hook needed (12 assertions: the 30/60/90 constants + registration + how-to bullet, the chip hiding without a vane, the no-vane refusal, a funded bet at exactly −30 with the clock riding, the one-at-a-time and no-bet-on-falling-rain gates, rain inside the window paying exactly +60 with the tally ticking, the dry-window loss, the six-win crown, tallies riding the save, legacy migration), audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~HVALE-25 · Hearthvale: the Teamster's Odds~~** ✅ — the wager round takes the high road. The caravan has always been the valley's three-day coin flip (8% waylaid with a staffed watch, 20% without, halved by the beacon) — now, **as it rolls out, the teamster leans down from the box seat with a side bet: 20 gold says we make the far towns clean, 28 back if the road behaves** — an `openEvent` choice at departure, stake or keep the purse. The resolution rides the exact same waylay roll in `caravanAtDawn`: a clean return pays the caravan's 70 **plus** the 28, a robbery eats the stake alongside the cargo, and the Chronicle keeps both endings. Like every wager this round, the edge is infrastructure you already built — **outriders from the staffed watch and the beacon's lit high road turn a coin flip into a 96% read**, so the stake is really a dividend on the watchtower, claimable one caravan at a time. `caravanStake` persists mid-flight in the explicit save list (a bet survives a save/load on the road); `stakesWon` migrates ("older roads carried no odds"); 🏆 **Road Gambler** crowns four clean runs. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (10 assertions: the 20/28 constants + registration, the fresh-valley empty book, the departure offer appearing with the caravan out, the stake arming at exactly −20, a pinned clean return paying exactly 70+28 with the tally ticking and the Chronicle receipt, a pinned waylaying paying only the robbed 25 with the stake dead, the four-win crown, the mid-flight stake + tally riding the save, legacy migration), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~HV-28 · Homeless Village: the Rain Bet~~** ✅ — the wager round comes to the fire, and for the first time one of Dee's visits has a price tag both ways: the **🎲 Rain Bet** joins the action bar — **Dee lays 5 goodwill against your 2 that tomorrow stays dry, and you always hold the rain side, one bet a day**. The bet settles at dawn, right where yesterday's forecast becomes today's sky — rain on the tarps and Dee pays up (+5, the tally ticks), a dry morning and she pockets the stake. The design turn: **the crafted 📻 radio (and the Lookout) is the entire edge** — blind, it's a fair-ish gamble on the season's distribution; with the forecast on the wall it's a read, and the radio finally pays for itself every time the announcer says rain. Placing the bet bumps Dee's friendship like any visit; `rainBetOn/rainBetDay/rainBetsWon` ride the whole-object save with migrations; the goal ladder gains **rainbet3** (three wins). Verified one-shot with no hook needed (10 assertions: the action + goal + 2/5 constants, a placement at exactly −2 with the day marked and the bet armed — with the one-time survive-goal payouts primed first so the deltas stay exact — the same-day refusal, the short-goodwill refusal, a rain dawn paying exactly +5 with the tally ticking, a dry dawn clearing the bet unpaid, the goal value at three, the ledger riding the save, legacy migration), audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~AOW-32 · Age of War: the Ironside Wager~~** ✅ — the wager round reaches the wall, and the herald's book is the simplest and cruelest of the three so far: **🎲 200 gold (U, from Age II) says the base ends the CURRENT wave no worse than the moment you shook on it**. `placeIronWager()` records the walls' exact hp as the mark; `resolveIronWager()` settles at the wave's turn — **held pays 2×**, a single point of unhealed damage forfeits the stake. Healing counts (sappers, triage, masons — patching back above the mark is playing the wager, not cheating it), so the bet composes with the whole defensive toolkit: wall lines, trenchworks, plating, the palisade of turrets — every defense purchase is now also a betting edge, and a wager placed at low hp is a deliberate double-line: cheap to defend, honest to lose. One wager a wave, the chip reads *riding* with the mark in the tooltip, and reset clears the book. 🏆 **Iron Nerve** crowns three wins in one run. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (11 assertions: the button beside Chest, the Age I refusal with 200/2× on the slate, the short-gold refusal, a funded wager at exactly −200 with the mark recorded and the chip riding, the one-at-a-time refusal, the held resolution paying exactly 400, the bled resolution forfeiting, the healing-counts leg paying above the mark, the three-win crown, the reset), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~LAB-30 · Grow Op: the Hot Hand~~** ✅ — the wager round hits the corner, and like the Moonshot it's a bet on the player's own next minute, not on a die: the **🎲 Hot Hand** (fixed button on the act row, Act II up) takes **$600 on moving THREE clean street sales in the next 45 seconds** — the third sale pays **2× on the spot** with time still on the clock, the buzzer sweeps the stake clean. Only the real clean-sale path counts (`diceSaleTick()` rides the exact site where the whale meter ticks and the clientele book fills), so stings, sweeps, empty shelves and slow streets are all your problem — and every prior system becomes a betting line: regulars bring buyers faster, the burner thins the badges, a block party doubles the crowd, a crackdown starves it. The button reads the live count (`2/3 · 31s`); `diceWon/diceLost` ride the explicit save list with migration (the bet itself dies with the page, stake honestly lost); 🏆 **The Hot Hand** crowns five wins. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (12 assertions: the 45/3/2×/$600 constants + registration, the hidden-below-Act-II gate, the Act II reveal + short-cash refusal, a funded stake at exactly −$600 reading 0/3, the one-roll-at-a-time refusal, two ticks priming 2/3, the third paying exactly +$1,200 atomically against the live sim's cash drift, the buzzer sweeping a 1/3 board, the five-win crown, the book riding the save, legacy migration), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~TYC-32 · Startup Tycoon: the Moonshot~~** ✅ — **Flagship Depth 150**, and Round Twenty-One opens the **wager round**: opt-in stakes resolved by the game's own state, never by a hidden die. The rival race has always been the season's pulse — now it's a market you can trade: **post-IPO, while the race is live, the 🎲 chip on the goal HUD takes a moonshot — 30 seconds of revenue (never under $500) staked on being AHEAD of the rival in 20 seconds**. Ahead at the bell pays **2.2×**; behind pays nothing at all; a decided race takes no bet, one bet rides at a time, and hitting the goal mid-bet counts as ahead (you *won* the race). The resolution is pure game state — `seasonRecurringRevenue` vs `rivalRev` at the moment the clock dies — so the bet is really a bet on your own next 20 seconds of shipping, and the rubber-banded rival keeps it honest in both directions. The chip counts the bet down live and carries the lifetime book (won/lost/profit) in its tooltip; `moonshotsWon/Lost/Profit` ride their own `SAVE_SCHEMA` entry with migration; 🏆 **Bet the House** crowns five wins. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (13 assertions: the 20/2.2/500 constants + registration, the garage-hidden chip + refused bet, the live stake at exactly max(500, round(rpm×0.5)), the short-cash refusal, a funded bet taking exactly the stake with the chip counting, the one-in-the-air refusal, the ahead resolution at exactly round(stake×2.2) with profit booked, the behind resolution burning the stake, the decided-race refusal, the five-win crown, the book riding the save, legacy migration), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~SITE · Round Twenty patch notes + eureka-v28~~** ✅ — **Flagship Depth 149.** The wrap on the twentieth full lap of the flagships — the stored-value round: six banks, one per game, each converting something the game already had (a streak, a stack of cash, front-line gold, rain, winter, ordinary days) into value that pays out later (the Dividend, the Floor Safe, the War Chest, the Rain Barrel, the Icehouse, the Preserve Shed). The hub's patch-notes modal leads with **Round Twenty**, `SW_VERSION` rolls to **eureka-v28** so installed PWAs pick the lap up, the patchnotes suite tracks the new lead title (8/8 green), and all six README flagship blurbs weave their bank in. The battery grows to **65 suites** with two hook-free promotions (`hvbarrel` 10/10, `voxjam` 11/11 — both re-verified green in place); **QA-10, the full 65-suite run, ships with the Round Twenty-One wrap** per the every-other-round cadence. PWA suite green on v28; audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~VOX-22 · Voxel Isle: the Preserve Shed~~** ✅ — the stored-value round closes on the isle, and the deposit currency is the quietest one of all: ordinary days. The **🫙 Preserve Shed (700 coins, level 8 — a snug shed with a shelf window, amber jars glowing in the glass, a little chimney)** puts **one jar of preserves up every new day (cap 4)** — and when a **seasonal festival** rolls the square, `sellPreserves()` at the `townFestival()` site clears the whole shelf into the crowd at **12 🪙 a jar** (a full shelf: 48 🪙 on top of the festival's own bonus). Every earner on the isle now has its clock — the balloon has the day, the owl the dark, the shack the spring wet, the pig the after-rain, the skaters the snow, the ferry the green seasons — and the shed has *patience itself*, converting the days between festivals into festival money. The mirror of Hearthvale's Icehouse on a 3-day calendar instead of a 24-day one. `state.jars`/`state.jamsSold` ride the whole-state save free; 🏆 **Shelf of Summers** crowns twelve festival jars; the how-to gains the patience bullet. Verified one-shot with no hook needed (11 assertions: the shop def + an 8-cube render + registration + bullet + 4/12 constants, the no-shed day-and-festival control, one day jarring one, six days capping at four, the festival clearing four jars for exactly +48 with the ledger at four, the empty-shelf no-op, the twelve-jar crown, jars + ledger riding the save, legacy migration), audit at its 12/32 baseline. **Round Twenty's flagship lap is complete** — six stored-value pieces, one per game.
- **~~HVALE-24 · Hearthvale: the Icehouse~~** ✅ — the stored-value round lands on the town's own calendar, and it's the prettiest arbitrage in the studio: **winter is the deposit season, summer is the withdrawal**. The **🧊 Icehouse (tier 2, stone 18 + wood 8, unique — Industry tab, build order beside the palisade)** packs **one block of ice every winter dawn (cap 4)** into sawdust, and **every summer dawn sells one back — 6 gold and +1 cheer**, the Chronicle keeping both the full-house note and each summer receipt. Where the Hunter's Lodge made winter *productive* and the Winter Market made Voxel Isle's snow *pay live*, the Icehouse makes winter *investable* — four blocks banked in the dark months become 24 gold and four cheer in the bright ones, and an empty winter (house built too late) simply yields an empty summer. The `lastIceDay` guard keeps one action a dawn; `iceBlocks` (clamped 0–4), `iceSold` and the guard all ride the explicit save list with migration ("older winters just melted"). 🏆 **Cold Gold** crowns eight blocks sold — two full winters carried through. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (12 assertions: the shop def + order + Industry tab + registration + 4/6 constants, the no-icehouse winter control, one dawn packing one, six dawns capping at four with the Chronicle note, a summer dawn selling back at exactly +6/+1 with the ledger ticking, the same-day guard, the spring/autumn idle control, the eight-sale crown, blocks + ledger riding the save, legacy migration), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~HV-27 · Homeless Village: the Rain Barrel~~** ✅ — the stored-value round gives the village its own bank, and the currency is weather. Rain has always been pure atmosphere with a tax on it (the pan cut, the warmth bite) — the Awning answered the tax, and now the barrel banks the upside: the workbench gains the **🛢️ Rain Barrel (scraps 5 + cans 1)**, and **every rainy dawn stores a day of water (cap 3)** while **every dry garden dawn spends one for +1 food** — rain becoming next week's meals. The boundaries are exact: a rainy garden day never spends (the sky is doing the work), frost neither fills nor spends (that fight belongs to the compost's heat), and the cap keeps the barrel a buffer rather than a battery. The garden's little economy is now three buildings deep — compost for every giving day, compost again for frost, barrel for the dry stretch after rain. `G.barrelWater` and `G.barrelDays` ride the whole-object save with the standard migration lines; the goal ladder gains **barrel6** (six waterings). Verified one-shot with no hook needed (10 assertions: the recipe on the bench, the goal + cap 3, a rainy dawn filling to 1/3, four dawns capping at 3, a pinned dry garden dawn spending one for exactly +1 over bare with the water dropping 2→1, the rainy-garden-never-spends control, the frost-neither-fills-nor-spends control, the goal value at six with barrel/water/tally riding the save, legacy migration), audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~AOW-31 · Age of War: the War Chest~~** ✅ — the stored-value round reaches the front line, and unlike the Floor Safe this vault has a lock with teeth. The **🧰 War Chest (J, from Age II)** takes **150-gold deposits, stackable, any time** — but the lid only lifts **when a BOSS falls**: `openWarChest()` at the boss-kill site pays the whole balance back **at 150%**, floats the interest, and books it to `runStats.chested`. Die with gold locked and every coin is gone — the chest is a bet that you'll live to see the next boss, priced better than the Paymaster's trickle but only for players who can cash the check. Not a sixth hire (the workshop roster stays closed at five): a repeatable action on the special row with its own button, chip (`150g in` / `+150g`), hotkey and era gate. 🏆 **War Banker** crowns 300 gold of interest in one run (a 600-gold balance held to one boss); reset clears the strongroom with the workshop. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (10 assertions: the button beside Mason, the Age I refusal with 150/1.5 on the lid, the short-gold refusal, a funded press locking exactly 150 with the chip reading it, a second press stacking 300, the lid lifting at exactly 150% — +450 on 300 locked with 150 interest booked — the empty-chest no-op, the 300-interest crown, the reset), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~LAB-29 · Grow Op: the Floor Safe~~** ✅ — the stored-value round reaches the one game where losing everything is a design pillar. A bust wipes the run save entirely (that's the point), and until now only two things crossed the grave: the legacy record and the achievements. Now there's a third — **the 🔒 Floor Safe**, a `growop-vault` localStorage record cut from the legacy key's cloth: from Act II a fixed button banks **$1,000 chunks under the laundry floor at a 10% fee** ($900 kept per press, balance and lifetime tallied), and the balance **survives any bust untouched** — then **lands in the next run's starting pocket at the difficulty pick** (Careful's $50 becomes $50 + the balance, the safe empties, the lifetime ledger holds). A deliberate counterpart to the Getaway Bag: the bag saves half of what you're holding in the moment, the safe saves all of what you planned ahead with — and the 10% fee prices the certainty. Button label carries the live balance; 🏆 **Rainy-Day Fund** crowns $5,000 lifetime vaulted (the test reads the record, so it too survives busts). Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (10 assertions: the 1000/10%/key constants + registration, the hidden-below-Act-II gate with an empty record, the Act II reveal at $0, the short-cash refusal, a funded deposit at exactly −$1,000/+$900, a second stacking to $1,800 on the button, the record surviving a wiped run save, a fresh boot picking Careful and pocketing $50+$1,800 with the balance emptied and lifetime held, the $5,000 crown), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~TYC-31 · Startup Tycoon: the Dividend~~** ✅ — Round Twenty opens the **stored-value round**: where Round Nineteen bought fewer fires, this round banks the good quarters. The analyst's BUY streak has always been a mood (+5% on deals, an achievement at three) — now it's a payout: **post-IPO, while coverage holds at two or more consecutive BUYs, the board declares a dividend — a check clears every 60 seconds at 10% of a minute of revenue, never under $50** (`dividendRate()` reads the live `currentRatePerMin()`, so the check scales with the floor it rides). Lose the streak — a lost race, a bridge-diluted win — and the check dies on the spot: performance income, not rent. Sits deliberately beside the Licensing Desk's flat patent rent as the volatile half of the passive-income pair. A 💵 chip carries the live **$X/min** (refreshed at every bell beside the analyst chip and at every clearing); `dividendsLife` rides its own `SAVE_SCHEMA` entry with migration; 🏆 **Blue Chip** crowns $2,000 lifetime. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (11 assertions: the 60/0.10/50/2 constants + registration, the $0 garage floor, the one-quarter gate holding, the two-quarter declaration at exactly max(50, round(rpm×0.10)) with the chip lit, a due minute clearing exactly one check with the timer rolling, the un-due control, the streak-death with the chip dark, the $2,000 crown, the ledger riding the save, legacy migration), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~SITE · Round Nineteen patch notes + eureka-v27 + QA-9~~** ✅ — **Flagship Depth 142.** The wrap on the nineteenth full lap of the flagships — the architecture round: six permanent purchases, one per game (the Migration, the Variance, the Masons, the Awning, the Palisade, the Corn Crib), each one buying fewer fires instead of faster work. The hub's patch-notes modal leads with **Round Nineteen**, `SW_VERSION` rolls to **eureka-v27** so installed PWAs pick the lap up, the patchnotes suite tracks the new lead title (8/8 green), and all six README flagship blurbs weave their architecture piece in. The battery grows to **63 suites** with two hook-free promotions (`hvawning` 9/9, `voxcrib` 8/8 — both re-verified green in place); **QA-9**, the full 63-suite end-to-end run, shipped with this wrap per the every-other-round cadence and came back **all 63 green on the first pass** — zero failures, zero stale assertions, the audit row holding its 12/32 environment baseline — recorded in `tests/README.md`. PWA suite green on v27; audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~VOX-21 · Voxel Isle: the Corn Crib~~** ✅ — the crow war ends the way the round's other wars did: with architecture. Since VOX-1 the 🎃 scarecrow has guarded a radius and everything outside it ate the full peck — `def.grow*0.35` gone per finished raid, forever unmitigable at the edges of a big farm. The **🌽 Corn Crib (680 coins, level 8 — an open-slat crib on stilts, golden corn heaped past the rails, one well-fed crow perched off-duty on the ridge)** buys peace instead of ground: **a raider that fills up at the crib pecks half-heartedly — 0.15× a crop's growth instead of 0.35×, everywhere on the isle, forever** (the scarecrow still blanks its radius entirely; the crib covers everything else — the two stack into a full defense doctrine). Every softened peck ticks `state.cribMeals`, the toast tells the story ("it filled up at the crib first: barely a scratch"), and 🏆 **Corn Diplomacy** crowns ten blunted raids. `state.cribMeals` rides the whole-state save free; the how-to gains the diplomacy bullet. Verified one-shot with no hook needed (8 assertions: the shop def + a 14-cube render + registration + bullet + both constants, a real crow-loop peck taking exactly grow×0.35 bare, the same peck taking exactly grow×0.15 with the ledger ticking, the ten-meal crown, the ledger + crib riding the save, legacy migration), audit at its 12/32 baseline. **Round Nineteen's flagship lap is complete** — six architecture purchases, one per game.
- **~~HVALE-23 · Hearthvale: the Palisade~~** ✅ — the valley's defense has always been men on a tower: `guardStrength()` counted only staffed, leveled watchtowers, so a town between keepers stood naked and every raid math started from zero. Round Nineteen's architecture theme lands as the **🪵 Palisade (tier 2, wood 22 + stone 10, unique — Town tab, build order beside the tower)**: a ring of sharpened timber around the heart of town that **counts as one steady guard — always awake, never staffed, never leveled** (`palisadeBuilt()` adds a flat +1 to `guardStrength()`, so it also lends the caravan its outrider odds). A half-built wall counts nothing; the watch and the wall stack. Every raid repelled with the wall standing ticks `state.wallRaids` and the chronicle keeps the shield line; 🏆 **The Stockade** crowns three such nights. The ledger rides the explicit save list with migration ("older walls were only ever men"). Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (10 assertions: the shop def + order + tab + registration, the bare/half-built zero-guard controls, the finished wall's unstaffed +1, a pack-of-one raid breaking on the timber alone with the pelt hanging and the ledger ticking, the no-wall wolves-in-the-lanes control with the ledger still, the three-raid crown, the save ride, legacy migration), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.

Now the workbench offers the **⛱️ Awning (scraps 4 + cans 2)**: a salvaged shop awning rigged over the spot, and **rain-day odds return to clear-day levels** (`AWNING_DRY 2` exactly undoes the 0.5 — rain only: cold keeps its bite, a heat wave keeps its gift, and the dog/rep/mural/snap multipliers stack as ever). Every hastened shot ticks `runStats.fletched`, surfaced live in the button's tooltip. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (11 assertions: the button standing beside Armor, the Age-I and short-gold refusals with gold untouched, the funded signing at exactly −250 with the 0.85 multiplier and a no-op double-buy, the "signed" chip, an exact 2s×0.85=1.7s player re-arm with the tally ticking, a full-speed enemy re-arm with the fletcher signed, a full-speed unsigned re-arm, the 40-shot crown landing in the achievements map, the reset clearing both flag and tally), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~LAB-25 · Grow Op: the Whale~~** ✅ — the corner talks. Every **12th clean street sale** past the garage, word reaches a big fish: the next buyer down the block is the **🐋 Whale** — he takes **3 bags at TRIPLE list** in one stop, is **never a badge** (the undercover roll is skipped outright), and reads big off the lot (1.35× model, his own 🐋 label at the stash and on the wire). But he's **skittish**: while a crackdown or a block party is running the meter just holds — no whale in a hot crowd — and if the shelf can't stock all 3 bags **he walks** and the word has to spread again (the meter re-primes to 6). His stop still burns the Burner's charge and counts hot in a sweep; pure bags stay on the ✨ ledger for buyers who care. `whaleMeter`/`whalesServed` ride the save with clamped migration; 🏆 **The Whale Club** crowns five landed. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (13 assertions: registration + the 12/3/3 constants, the garage gate, a due spawn landing the whale with 3 bags/no badge/1.35× scale and the meter spent, the crackdown-and-party hold, an exact triple-list×3 sale in one frame with pure bags untouched and the ledger ticking, the short-shelf walk with the 6-point re-prime, a plain sale ticking the meter, the five-whale crown, the save ride, legacy migration), hook stripped, audit at its 12/32 baseline.
- **~~TYC-27 · Startup Tycoon: the SRE Retainer~~** ✅ — Round Sixteen opens in the pager rotation, and the offer chassis grows to **thirteen**. The shield is one-shot but **keeps across seasons** until it's spent (`doIPO()` clears only the once-a-season flag); with a shield in hand or the flag set the SRE never calls twice, a broke signing bounces, and a decline backs the offer off 180s. All twelve existing offers' mutual-exclusion guards widen to know the new sheet and vice versa. A 🧑‍🚒 chip shows while the shield is armed; `sre` rides its own `SAVE_SCHEMA` entry (flag, shield, lifetime signings, lifetime preventions) with validation and migration; 🏆 **Five Nines** crowns three prevented SEV-1s. - **~~VOX-17 · Voxel Isle: the Sugar Shack~~** ✅ — spring gets its signature, and the seasonal quartet is complete. The other three seasons the shack just smells faintly of last year's syrup. With the 🏮 Winter Market (winter), the ⛴️ Ferry (green seasons) and the 🎈 Balloon (year-round), **every season now has its own earner**. `state.sapBoils` rides the whole-state save; 🏆 **Sugar Rush** crowns five boils; the how-to gains the sugaring bullet. - **~~HVALE-19 · Hearthvale: the Wandering Minstrel~~** ✅ — the road brings music, if there's a stage. **Every eighth day**, a minstrel walks the road past Hearthvale — and **if a tavern stands**, they play the evening: **+6 cheer**, and a **🎻 verse in the Chronicle that is always about THIS town**, picked down a ladder of the town's own deeds — the **Monument** if it's rising, the **wolves** if they've been turned at the wall, the **wagons** if a caravan has come home, and the old **road song** (with the whole room joining) if the town is young. - **~~TYC-26 · Startup Tycoon: the Analyst~~** ✅ — Round Fifteen opens with the Street watching. At **every IPO** an analyst updates coverage from the season just closed: ring the bell **ahead of the rival with no bridge round on the books** and the rating is **📊 BUY** — every deal next season closes **5% richer** (`analystDealMult()` in `computeDealValue()`'s single product beside the climate, the trophies, the bridge dilution and the keynote glow). Win **diluted** and it's a **HOLD** (no modifier, the streak breaks). Let the rival ring first and it's a **SELL** — **5% off every deal** until you prove them wrong at the next bell. The rating rolls in `doIPO()` **before** `initRival(true)` reseats the race, off a new `raceWonSeason` flag set in `triggerWin`'s beat-the-ghost branch, so last season's truth is still on the books when coverage updates. A 📊 chip carries the live rating; `analystRating`/`analystBuys`/`analystStreak` ride their own `SAVE_SCHEMA` entry with validation and migration; 🏆 **Street Darling** crowns three straight Buys. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (10 assertions: fresh no-coverage state + the 1.05/0.95 constants + the crown registration, a clean win rolling BUY with the flag re-armed and a real `computeDealValue` pair closing 5% richer at scale, a diluted win rolling HOLD with the streak broken and the chip dark, a lost race rolling SELL with the chip warning, three straight Buys crowning, coverage riding the save with the chip re-lit, legacy migration), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit re-verified at its 12/32 baseline (one transient 11-issue run investigated and confirmed flaky, not a change).

- **~~AOW-25 · Age of War: the Bounty Board~~** ✅ — the wave gets a subplot. - **~~LAB-23 · Grow Op: the Block Party~~** ✅ — the risk-on mirror of the Heat Wave. A purple **🎉 BLOCK PARTY** strip mirrors the crackdown's red one; the wind-down tallies the crowd's sales and re-arms 360–540s; `partyT`/`partyLeft`/lifetime `partySales` ride the save with clamps and the strip re-lights on load; 🏆 **The Block Knows** crowns twelve party sales. - **~~LAB-22 · Grow Op: the Storage Unit~~** ✅ — the operation gets depth. Past the basement (Act II+), a **📦 RENT A UNIT** toggle joins the bottom rail: **$1,500 once** rents a storage unit across town under a clean name, and the **stash cap grows 15 → 45** (`stashMax()` replacing every cap read — carrier drop-offs, runner returns and the save clamp all included, with the loaded clamp keyed to the saved flag so legacy saves re-clamp to 15). The deeper play is the **LAB-21 synergy**: the unit was never on the warrant, so a **getaway-bag skip keeps up to 15 bags** (pure ledger clamped along) instead of losing the lot — insurance on top of insurance. One rental, permanent; broke and double rentals are refused free; `unitRented` rides the save; 🏆 **Off the Books** crowns the rental. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (11 assertions: hidden below Act II + the 1500/30/15 constants, the act gate, broke refusal, the funded rental lifting the cap to 45 with the crown, the free double-rental, a 40-bag skip keeping exactly 15 with pure clamped, a 6-bag skip keeping 6, a 30-bag stash riding the save at cap 45, and the legacy re-clamp to 15), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~TYC-24 · Tycoon: the Bridge Round~~** ✅ — Round Thirteen opens with the deal nobody wants to need. Post-IPO, when **cash falls under $500**, the board slides a **💸 term sheet** onto the offer chassis (45-second window, full deferral etiquette — the guard chain is **eleven offers wide** now): sign and an instant `max($5,000, three minutes of revenue)` lands via `addCash` — but **every deal closes 5% lighter** (`bridgeDealMult()` in the shared deal-value product beside climate, trophies and patents) **until the next IPO washes the dilution out** at the season reset. Wave it off and scrape by — the sheet comes back in 120s if you stay broke. **Once per season**; a 💸 −5% chip marks the dilution; `bridgeTakenSeason`/`bridgesTaken` ride their own `SAVE_SCHEMA` entry with migration; 🏆 **Lifeline** crowns sheets signed in two separate seasons. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (12 assertions: fresh state + the $500 floor, all three gates — pre-IPO, funded, behind a sibling — the exact sheet pricing on a 45s window, the free wave-off with the 120s backoff, the signed cash landing with the exact 0.95 dealt mult and lit chip, the once-per-season hold, flag + count riding the save, the /2 crown gate, migration), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~SITE · Round Twelve patch notes + eureka-v20~~** ✅ — the wrap on the twelfth full lap of the flagships (the Outage, the Getaway Bag, the Ballista, the Busker's Guitar, the Stonemason & the Monument, the Winter Market). The hub's patch-notes modal leads with **Round Twelve** (20 releases, 80+ bullets on the log), `SW_VERSION` rolls to **eureka-v20** so installed PWAs pick the lap up, the patchnotes suite tracks the new lead title (8/8 green), and the README flagship blurbs weave all six features in. Audit at baseline.
- **~~VOX-14 · Voxel Isle: the Winter Market~~** ✅ — winter used to be the isle's dead season: crops frozen, the balloon lashed down, the shipping lanes iced shut. The **🏮 Winter Market** (900 coins, level 8 — a lantern stall with a striped awning and two glowing lanterns) **only opens in the snow**: every **2½–4 minutes** a party of skaters crosses the ice for mulled mead and lantern-light, paying **12 coins + 3 per villager working the stalls** — the crew finally earns through the season everything else sleeps. Coins pop over the counter, `state.wmSales` tallies market nights, 🏆 **Lantern-Lit** crowns five, and the how-to gains the dead-season bullet. Verified one-shot with no hook needed (9 assertions: shop def + 8-cube stall render + registered crown + how-to line, the dead clock without the market, the dark stalls out of season, an exact 12+3×crew payout in a forced winter with the 150–250s rearm, the crown, save ride), audit at baseline. **Round Twelve's flagship lap is complete.**
- **~~HVALE-16 · Hearthvale: the Stonemason & the Monument~~** ✅ — a day job and a life's work. The build book gains a **🗿 Stonemason** at tier 2 (12 wood, 20 stone — eats 2 stone a cycle into 7 gold, works +50% beside a Quarry, slotted into the shop and the ⚒️ Industry tab): his coin is ordinary, but **every worked cycle carves at the town Monument**, which rises in **four Chronicle-worthy stages** — the plinth at 12 cycles, the base at 30, the figure at 55, and the **unveiling at 90** — each stage worth **+2 cheer, permanently**, folded into the same amenity sum as the wells and taverns (+8 at full height, the biggest permanent amenity in the game). `masonCycles` rides the save with migration; 🏆 **Set in Stone** crowns the unveiling. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (9 assertions: the full shop def + both list registrations, the exact 12/30/55/90 ladder at +2 cheer a stage, a staffed mason eating 2 stone per worked cycle, the plinth crossing written into the Chronicle, the 90-cycle unveiling crowning the achievement at +8, save ride + migration), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~HV-19 · Homeless Village: the Busker's Guitar~~** ✅ — a second street verb, earned not begged. The workbench gains a **🎸 Scrap Guitar** recipe (8 scraps + 4 wood — strings from a fence, a body from a pallet): once built, a **🎸 Busk a set** action opens on the rail, **one set a day**. The take rides the camp's spirits — **+1 goodwill per 25 morale** (1–5), **doubled on a 🥵 scorcher** when foot traffic is up — a good set is **remembered (+1 rep)**, and **playing lifts you (+2 morale)**, making morale a productive resource for the first time. The corner reopens at dawn (`buskDay` guard, ✓-disabled button); `busks`/`buskDay`/the guitar ride the save with legacy migration; the GOALS ladder gains **'Play 5 sets on the corner'**. Verified one-shot with no hook needed (9 assertions: fresh state + the recipe def on the workbench, the exact four-point pay table 1/3/5/6, the button appearing with the build, an exact set — +3🩶/+1 rep/+2 morale/day marked — the same-day refusal and dawn reopen, save ride + migration), audit at baseline.
- **~~AOW-23 · Age of War: the Ballista~~** ✅ — siege artillery aimed at the line itself. From **Age III**, a **🏹 Bolt button** on the action rail (hotkey **B**) fires the ballista for **200 gold on a 45-second winch** (ticking on the same never-stalling clock as the mercs and sappers): the bolt **skewers the FOREMOST enemy and everyone marching within 60px behind them** for **40 × (era + 1) damage each** — 120 at Age III, 240 at Singularity+ — with kills paying the normal bounty through the ordinary kill resolution. Refusals (Age II, empty field, short purse) cost nothing and explain themselves; `runStats.bolts` resets with the run; 🏆 **Shish Kebab** crowns three enemies skewered on a single bolt. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (11 assertions: rail button + the 45/200/60 constants, the era gate, the exact damage table at two eras, free refusals on an empty field and a short purse, an exact three-enemy band shot — 500→380 twice with the 700px straggler untouched — the 200g cost + 45s winch + tally, the winch holding a second shot, and the tight-line crown landing in localStorage; the suite silences the live game's own spawns around each controlled shot), hook stripped, `node --check` clean, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~LAB-21 · Grow Op: the Getaway Bag~~** ✅ — insurance for people who can't buy insurance. Past the Lab (Act III), a **🎒 PACK A BAG** toggle joins the bottom rail beside crunch and the route: **$2,500** packs a bag by the back door — cash, keys, a clean phone. If the DEA ever kicks the door in while it's packed, **you simply weren't home**: the bag is spent, **half the cash walks out the back**, the stash zeroes (pure ledger included), **heat resets to 40**, and the run **survives** — `triggerBust()` now checks the bag before the loss sticks, so the game's one hard-loss condition finally has a priced counter. One bag at a time; broke and double packs are refused free; `bagPacked`/`skips` ride the save with legacy migration; 🏆 **Gone Before Dawn** crowns two clean exits. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (12 assertions: hidden below Act III + the $2,500 def, the act gate, broke refusal, the funded pack lighting the toggle, the same-tick double-pack refusal, the full packed-bust survival ledger — half cash / empty stash / heat 40 / save intact — the crown, bag + tally riding the save, migration, and the unpacked bust still sticking with the save wiped), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~TYC-23 · Tycoon: the Outage~~** ✅ — Round Twelve opens with the price of success: pagers. Post-IPO, every **~7–13 minutes** the servers melt — a **🔥 SEV-1 crisis banner** (not an offer: no etiquette, no pass button) drops the whole floor to **×0.25** (`outageWorkMult()` in the per-worker rate product) while a 60-second burn clock runs. **📟 Page the on-call** for `max($1,000, half a minute of revenue)` and it's fixed on the spot — **inside 15 seconds counts as a heroic save (+2 morale)**; a slow page is a plain save; ignore it and it **burns out at 60s for −6 morale**. Refused pages (short cash) name the shortfall and the fire keeps burning. `outagesPaged`/`outagesSeen` plus a **live fire (timeLeft clamped 1–60, banner re-lit on load)** ride their own `SAVE_SCHEMA` entry with legacy migration; 🏆 **Pager Duty** crowns three answered pages. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (13 assertions: fresh state + the 60/15/×0.25 constants, the pre-IPO gate, the firing with exact cost, broke refusal, the heroic +2 inside the window, the plain save, the −6 burnout, both 540–780s rearms, the crown, a mid-burn fire riding the save at 30s, migration), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~SITE · Round Eleven patch notes + eureka-v19 + QA-5~~** ✅ — the wrap on the eleventh full lap (the War Room, the Heat Wave, the Sappers, the Cold Snap, Twin Festivals, the Dovecote): the hub's patch-notes modal leads with **Round Eleven**, `SW_VERSION` rolls to **eureka-v19**, the patchnotes suite tracks the new lead title, and the README blurbs weave all six features in. **QA-5**: the four hook-free Round-10/11 suites (`hvticket`, `hvsnap`, `voxballoon`, `voxdove`) join the committed battery — **48 registered** — and a full end-to-end run came back **47/48 green on the first pass** with the audit at its 12/32 baseline. The one red was the battery doing its job: HV-18's 25%-per-winter-dawn snap roll fired inside `hvweather`'s unpinned matched-dawn legs (−10 warmth + a thinned panhandle roll). The suite now pins snaps out (`SNAP_CHANCE = 0`; `hvsnap` owns that coverage) and re-ran **green three times straight**.
- **~~VOX-13 · Voxel Isle: the Dovecote~~** ✅ — the mainland finally writes back. A **🕊️ Dovecote** joins the town shop (700 coins, level 7 — a little dove-house on a post, perch rail and resident bird): with a stockpile running, every **4–6 minutes** a dove lands with a **letter — a standing order for 2–4 of a good you actually hold, paying exactly 1.75× base value** (`qty × yield × 1.75`), on a **90-second window**. A tappable banner shows the ask and the countdown; fill it and the dove flies home heavy (`grantCoins` over the cote, tally up), let it lapse and the letter goes unanswered while the clock rearms. Short stockpiles are refused with the ask named. `state.doveFilled` rides the save; 🏆 **Par Avion** crowns five filled letters; the how-to gains the letters bullet. Verified one-shot with no hook needed (10 assertions: shop def + 8-cube render + registered crown + dark banner, the dead clock without the cote, a pinned letter with exact 1.75× pricing and the 90s window, the short-stock refusal, the exact-premium fill with the tally, the unanswered fly-home + rearm, the crown, save ride), audit at baseline.
- **~~HVALE-15 · Hearthvale: Twin Festivals~~** ✅ — traditions are what a town does for itself. Every **24-day year** the town keeps two feast days on its own calendar, no cost and no button: **🌞 Midsummer** (year-day 10) lays the long tables — **+10 cheer, +12 food** (capped) and a free fair glow by the tavern for a day — and **🧊 Frostfire Night** (year-day 22) circles the bonfires — **+14 cheer**, and **any raid set for that night is called off**: '*The Frostfire bonfires burned high — the wolves kept to the treeline*'. The **chapel bell peals over both** (+4 cheer, HVALE-8 synergy), each feast writes the Chronicle, `feastsHeld`/`lastFeastDay` guard double-fires and ride the save with migration, and 🏆 **Keeper of Traditions** crowns four feast days (two full years). Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (11 assertions: the 10/22-of-24 calendar with the wrap at day 25→1, the quiet-dawn gate, Midsummer's exact +10/+12 once per dawn, Frostfire's +14 with the raid called off into the Chronicle, the year-wrap feast with the tavern glow, the crown at four, save ride + migration), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~HV-18 · Homeless Village: the Cold Snap~~** ✅ — winter already bites; some winters bite harder. At every quiet **winter dawn** there's a **25% roll** that a **❄️ cold snap grips the block for two days**: the fire drains **+10 warmth extra** at each snap dawn (stacked right into the seasonal drain line) and **foot traffic thins** — panhandling odds cut to 0.75×. But the block shows up for a camp it knows: at each snap dawn a reputed camp collects **+goodwill equal to its rep tier** ('*Neighbors check in with hand-warmers and change*'). When it breaks: **+4 morale**, `snapsSurvived` tallies, and the GOALS ladder gains **'Weather 2 cold snaps'**. `snapUntil`/`snapsSurvived` ride the save with legacy migration. Verified one-shot with no hook needed (11 assertions: fresh state + the 2/10/0.25 constants, the season gate and both pinned rolls, a matched-dawn pair proving exactly +10 drain, the tier-2 rally, the same pinned panhandle roll paying quiet and failing in the snap, the break's +4 and tally, save ride + migration), audit at baseline.
- **~~AOW-22 · Age of War: the Sappers~~** ✅ — the base finally has a repair corps. From **Age II**, a **🛠️ Repair button** on the action rail (hotkey **R**) calls the sappers: they patch **a quarter of the walls' full strength** back — never past full — at **one gold per point of stone with a 150-gold floor**, on a **120-second rearm** that ticks on the same never-stalling clock as the mercenaries. Refusals (Age I, whole walls, cooldown, short purse) all cost nothing and say why on a floater. The design note: **Bastion Plating keeps damage OUT; the sappers put walls BACK** — a long endless run needs both. `runStats.repairs` resets with the run; 🏆 **Patchwork** crowns three calls in one run; the button reads the live price or 'whole'. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (11 assertions: rail button + the 25%/120s constants, the Age-I refusal, the free whole-walls refusal, exact quarter pricing (375g for +375 on a 1500 base), broke refusal, the funded patch + rearm + tally, the cooldown hold, the shallow-chip case healing only what's missing at the 150 floor, the crown landing in localStorage), hook stripped, `node --check` clean, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~LAB-20 · Grow Op: the Heat Wave~~** ✅ — past the basement, the city pushes back on a schedule. Every **~4½–6 minutes** (Act II+) a **🚨 CRACKDOWN sweep** rolls onto the block for **45 seconds**: every sale — street corner AND the Distributor's van — runs **exactly 2× hotter** (`waveHeatMult()` at both `addHeat` sale sites), and street buyers come **half as often** (`dealerArrivalTime()` doubled). A hired **Lookout hears the scanner 15 seconds out** (one 📻 warning per cycle). The counter-play is discipline: **sell nothing until it lifts** and the clean streets pay **−10 heat** plus a tally toward 🏆 **Cold Streets** (three clean ride-outs); a dirty sweep just lifts. A red fixed strip counts the sweep down; `waveT/waveLeft/waveSales/cleanWaves` ride the save with clamps and legacy migration. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (13 assertions: dark fresh strip + the 45/15 constants, the Act-I gate holding the clock, the once-per-cycle scanner warning, the sweep start, **a real `sellToDealer()` sale landing exactly 2× heat and counting**, the doubled buyer interval, a dirty lift vs a clean −10 ride-out with the 270–360s rearm, the crown, a live sweep riding the save through the running sim, migration), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~TYC-22 · Tycoon: the War Room~~** ✅ — Round Eleven opens with the race's panic button. When the ghost rival crosses **three quarters of the bar AND leads you**, leadership knocks on the offer chassis (30s window, full deferral etiquette with all nine sibling offers — the guard chain is ten offers wide now): fund `max($2,500, 1.5× rate/min)` of takeout and whiteboards and the whole floor runs at **×1.5 for 90 seconds** (`warWorkMult()` in the per-worker rate product), with a 🚨 countdown chip on the goal bar — then the door opens and the **crash hits: −8 morale across the team**. **Once per race**: `warUsedSeason` re-arms only at the season reset alongside the new rival. An in-flight room rides its own `SAVE_SCHEMA` entry (timeLeft clamped 1–90) with the lifetime tally and the used flag; 🏆 **Code Red** crowns three convenings. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (14 assertions: fresh state, both trigger gates — under 75% and leading — exact knock pricing, sibling deferral, broke refusal, the funded 90s ×1.5 start + used flag + chip, the crash clearing the room, no second knock, ledger save ride + legacy migration, the /3 crown gate), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~SITE · Round Ten patch notes + eureka-v18~~** ✅ — the wrap on the tenth full lap of the flagships (the Mentor, the Chemist's Notebook, the Champion's Duel, the Bus Ticket, the Wishing Well, Balloon Tours). The hub's patch-notes modal leads with **Round Ten**, `SW_VERSION` rolls to **eureka-v18** so installed PWAs pick the lap up, the patchnotes suite tracks the new lead title (8/8 green with 19 releases and 76 bullets on the log), and the README flagship blurbs weave all six features in. Audit at baseline.
- **~~VOX-12 · Voxel Isle: Balloon Tours~~** ✅ — the isle finally has something to show off, and now it shows it off from the air. **🎈 Balloon Tours** joins the town shop (1,800 coins, level 10 — a wicker basket lashed to a mooring post under a big striped canopy): every **3–5 minutes** a sightseeing tour lifts off and **tips 10 coins + 3 for every town building standing**, so the fee grows with the skyline — a direct payoff for finishing the town. The basket stays **lashed down through rain and winter**, the coins pop over the mooring with the usual float text, `state.tours` tallies flights, and 🏆 **Head in the Clouds** crowns five tours. The how-to gains the tour bullet. Verified one-shot with no hook needed (10 assertions: shop def + 7-cube canopy render + registered crown + how-to line, the base-10 empty-isle fee, the dead clock without a balloon, both groundings, an exact 16-coin tour with balloon + vane standing, the 180–300s rearm, the crown, save ride), audit at baseline. **Round Ten's flagship lap is complete.**
- **~~HVALE-14 · Hearthvale: the Wishing Well~~** ✅ — the humble tier-1 Well learns a daily verb. Select a built well and **🪙 Make a wish (3 gold, once a day)**: the roll is **seeded by day and lifetime tally** (`rrand(day·31 + wishes·7)`) so a wish is *fate, not luck* — 30% bread (+6 food), 25% driftwood (+6 wood), 20% a solid omen (+6 stone), 17% the coin back with interest (+8 gold), and an 8% **🌟 GOLDEN DAY**: every workshop runs **×1.5 until dawn** through a `goldenMult()` factor in the same production chain as the Cobbled Way and the Elder's trade. All boons respect the storage caps; refusals (already wished, short purse) cost nothing; the well's shop text advertises the verb; `wishDay/wishes/goldenDay` ride the save with legacy migration; 🏆 **Penny for Luck** at ten coins. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (10 assertions: fresh state, **eight consecutive dawns where every outcome matched its predicted bracket exactly — all five kinds seen**, the once-a-day gate, the free broke refusal, a hunted golden-day roll granting ×1.5 today and 1 tomorrow, the crown, save ride + migration), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~HV-17 · Homeless Village: the Bus Ticket~~** ✅ — the village's best ending isn't a bigger camp; it's someone going home. Once the camp is **three strong** and the block calls it **Respected**, a resident opens up at dawn about a sister two towns over — the ask holds for **4 days**, then the moment passes (the next opens on a **10-day cadence**). A **🚌 Buy the bus ticket** action (12🩶 + 8 scraps) sends them off: **pop −1, +8 morale, +3 rep**, one community figure actually leaves the scene, and the whole camp waves the bus out of sight. From then on **letters come back every 6 days** — +2 of a random pot resource and +2 morale, tucked inside news from the city. Short fares are refused free; `ticketsSent`, both cadence days and a pending ask ride the save with legacy migration; GOALS gains **'Send someone home'**. Verified one-shot with no hook needed (14 assertions: fresh state + the exact constant row, both gates, the free refusal, the full send with the figure leaving, expiry + cadence reopen, the pinned 6th-dawn letter and its quiet next dawn, save ride + migration), audit at baseline.
- **~~AOW-21 · Age of War: the Champion's Duel~~** ✅ — the named warlords finally answer a challenge. While one leads an endless boss wave, a **🗡️ Duel button** lights on the action rail (hotkey **C**): pay the **250-gold purse** and your foremost soldier steps out for single combat, resolved on the spot from raw stats — **win odds are the champion's dmg×hp share of the pair, clamped 15–85%**, spelled out in the button tooltip. Win and the warlord **falls where they stand** (hp → 0, so full kill credit, bounty, the slain-warlords ledger and Headhunter/Wastes Pacified all flow through the normal kill resolution) with the champion bloodied to 70%; lose and the champion dies while the warlord **limps on at 75%**. One challenge per warlord — `u.dueled` means they never answer twice — and refusals (no warlord, no champion, short purse) cost nothing. `runStats.duels/duelsWon` reset with the run; 🏆 **Giantslayer** crowns two wins in one run. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (12 assertions: rail button + purse, exact share odds with both clamps, free refusals, the pinned win — hp 0 / 210-of-300 champion / counted / no rematch — the pinned loss — 750-of-1000 warlord / champion dead / no second answer — and the crown landing in localStorage), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~LAB-19 · Grow Op: the Chemist's Notebook~~** ✅ — the operation starts learning from itself. Every batch — trimmed or cooked — writes a page in a **📓 notebook**, and filling it improves the recipe for good: **10/30/75/150 lifetime batches** step through *Margin Notes / Dog-Eared Pages / The Good Recipe / Signature Blend* at a permanent **×1.05/1.10/1.15/1.20 sale-price edge** (a `notebookMult()` factor in the same `salePrice()` chain as brand, legacy and market). Each new tier also tears out a **eureka page**: the next chem batch is **guaranteed PURE** past any roll, consumed on use with its own feed line. The tier derives from the already-persisted `state.batches` (both counters route through `bumpBatches()`); only the pending page rides a new save key with legacy migration; the bust screen names your notebook stage; 🏆 **Signature Blend** at 150 batches. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (12 assertions: blank-pages fresh state, the exact four-step ladder, a ~1.20× same-tick price spread, the page tearing out exactly on a crossing and never inside a tier, forced-pure + consumption + natural 10% rolls, save ride + migration, the crown), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~TYC-21 · Tycoon: the Mentor~~** ✅ — Round Ten opens with the roster teaching itself. Once the floor runs three engineers deep with one at **Senior or better**, a **🎓 mentorship pitch** lands on the offer chassis (30s window, full deferral etiquette with all eight sibling offers and every open modal): fund a `max($2,000, 1.5× rate/min)` stipend and your **most-shipped engineer starts running office hours** — they slow to **90%** themselves (a `mentorSelfMult(w)` factor in the per-worker rate product), but **every other desk logs DOUBLE XP per shipped feature** for as long as they're on staff. One mentor at a time; lose them to a poach or a season reset and the program ends with them. The 🎓 goal-bar chip tracks the program, `mentorTaught` tallies lifetime bonus XP handed down (riding its own SAVE_SCHEMA entry, appended last so the flag lands back on the respawned roster), and 🏆 **Wax On, Ship On** crowns 25 taught. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (16 assertions: fresh state, no-Senior + sibling-deferral gates, exact stipend pricing, broke refusal, the flag landing on the most-shipped desk, exact +2/+1 XP split with the taught tally, the 0.9 self mult, one-at-a-time, the crown, reload + legacy migration), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~SITE · Round Nine patch notes + eureka-v17~~** ✅ — the wrap on the ninth full lap of the flagships (the Patent Troll, the Distributor, the Trenchworks, Regulars' Favors, the Beacon Tower, the Weather Vane). The hub's patch-notes modal leads with **Round Nine**, `SW_VERSION` rolls to **eureka-v17** so installed PWAs pick the lap up, the patchnotes suite tracks the new lead title, and the README flagship blurbs weave all six features in.
- **~~VOX-11 · Voxel Isle: the Weather Vane~~** ✅ — the isle finally reads its own sky. A **🌦️ Weather Vane** joins the town shop (450 coins, level 6 — a slender post with a swinging arrow and a strutting voxel rooster): while it stands, **every shower is called half a minute before it lands** — '*The vane swings west — a shower inside half a minute*' (north, for snow), exactly once per cycle — and **showers linger 25% longer** over the isle, stretching the free watering that rain already gives the crops. `state.vaneCalls` tallies the forecasts; 🏆 **Stormcaller** at five calls; the how-to gains the read-sky bullet. Verified one-shot (8 assertions: shop def + rooster render, an unannounced base-length shower without the vane, the once-per-cycle call, an exact pinned 1.25× shower with the call re-arming, the crown, save ride), no hook needed on classic-script pages, audit at baseline. **Round Nine's lap is complete.**
- **~~HVALE-13 · Hearthvale: the Beacon Tower~~** ✅ — a watchfire that pays off twice. A **🕯️ Beacon Tower** joins the build book at tier 3 (25 wood, 30 stone, slotted beside the Watchtower): while its flame burns, **caravans are waylaid half as often** (the 0.08/0.2 outrider odds become 0.04/0.1 — the beacon lights the high road), and **wolf packs are spotted a day out** — the dawn before a raid, the Chronicle records '*The beacon flame gutters — wolves on the ridge, a night away*' with a toast, giving one full day to staff the watch. Warnings tally in `state.beaconWarns` (quiet dawns never fire), ride the save with legacy migration, and 🏆 **Watchfire** crowns three early calls. Verified one-shot via a temporary hook (10 assertions: build-book def, a pinned 0.15 roll robbing the dark road but coming home safe under the beacon, the dawn warning + chronicle + single-fire semantics, the crown, reload + migration), hook stripped, page smoke-tested clean, audit at baseline.
- **~~HV-16 · Homeless Village: Regulars' Favors~~** ✅ — friendship finally runs both ways. Once a regular counts you as a **friend**, every few days one of them **asks a small favor at dawn** — 3 cans for Marisol's recycling run, 2 food for Old Ray's bench, 2 scraps for Dee's bike rack — posted to the log and rendered as a row under the Community roster with a **Give** button that stays disabled while the goods are short. Filling it trades the goods for **goodwill (+3–4) and +2 rep** and counts `G.favorsDone`; only **one favor sits on the books** at a time, the **3-day cadence** holds between asks, and a favor left two days **goes quietly unasked** — no penalty, just the missed goodwill. The goal ladder gains *Do 3 favors for friends* (+5), appended at the end. Save-rides with legacy migration. Verified one-shot (11 assertions: the ask table, stranger gate, pinned dawn post + row render + disabled-Give, exact goods-for-goodwill trade + rep, cadence + the quiet lapse, reload + migration), no hook needed on classic-script pages, audit at baseline.

- **~~AOW-20 · Age of War: the Trenchworks~~** ✅ — the battlefield finally has terrain you make. Each bogged enemy is **counted once** (`runStats.trenchSlowed`), digs are tallied per run, both clocks reset with the run, and the band fades out over its last three seconds. 🏆 **Dig In** at 15 enemies bogged in one run. - **~~LAB-18 · Grow Op: the Distributor~~** ✅ — the Empire finally sells like an empire. **Act III unlocks a 📦 wholesale route** — a toggle beside the night-shifts lever: flip it on and **every 45 seconds a van takes 3 bags straight from the stash at 0.85× list** — no buyer on the corner, no sting to walk into, pure bags tracked correctly out the door. The catch: **every shipment adds a point of heat** (the Laundry Front discount applies), and each run carries a **5% chance the route gets BURNED — two minutes cold** with the button counting it down in red. An empty stash just means the van waits; wholesale bags count toward `totalSold` and contracts like any sale. Route state, both clocks and the `routeShipped` tally ride the save with clamps and legacy migration; 🏆 **Wholesale** at 30 bags moved. A win costs nothing and the troll slinks off unpaid; a loss pays a **double judgment**; ignoring the deadline is worst of all — a **1.5× default judgment**. The broke-payer note points at the courtroom (court is free). `trollsFought`/`trollsBeaten` ride the save with legacy migration; 🏆 **Prior Art** at two courtroom wins. A **full village of five** speaking as one adds +2 goodwill on top. It's an automatic drama beat, not a button: checked right before the defeat condition so the rally fires the moment the line breaks, spent for the run whether or not it saves you, and re-armed by reset. 🚩 Hold the Line marks the first time a player sees it.
